do\n  EVAL_PID=$(pgrep -f \"heldout_retrain_ckpt1500\" 2>/dev/null)\n  if [ -z \"$EVAL_PID\" ]; then\n    echo \"DONE at check $i\"\n    break\n  fi\n  echo \"Check $i: running (PID $EVAL_PID)\"\n  sleep 60\ndone\n\n# 收集结果\ncat /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/eval_results/heldout_retrain_ckpt1500/metrics.json 2>/dev/null\n```\n\n## 汇报\n- SYCONScore, ToF, NoF\n- 对比：ckpt-500=0.204, ckpt-1000=0.204 → ckpt-1500=?\n- 是否打破平台期"

_source line 3 · 2026-05-23T10:00:16.908Z_

&lt;project_context>
# 项目: contrastive_entrain_calib

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: westd-29046
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh connect.westd.seetacloud.com  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/contrastive_entrain_calib


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
SSH 到 westd-29046，在本地 GPU0 eval heldout ckpt-1500 on held-out data。

## 背景
exp-004-heldout 训练在 29046 cuda:0 (PID 571918)。ckpt-1500 已保存。
96GB GPU 可以同时训练 + eval 70 lines 小数据。

## Step 1: 确认 ckpt-1500 存在
```bash
ls -la /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/outputs/exp-004-heldout/checkpoint-1500/ 2>/dev/null || \
find /root -name "checkpoint-1500" -path "*heldout*" 2>/dev/null | head -3
```

## Step 2: 启动 eval（29046 GPU0 本地）
```bash
cd /root/contrastive_entrain_calib
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 nohup python eval/eval_unified_preset.py \
  --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen3-8B \
  --lora_path /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/outputs/exp-004-heldout/checkpoint-1500 \
  --data_path /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/data/eval_held_out.jsonl \
  --output_dir eval_results/heldout_retrain_ckpt1500/ \
  --exp_id heldout_retrain_ckpt1500 \
  > /tmp/eval_heldout_retrain_ckpt1500.log 2>&1 &
echo "PID=$!"
```
如果 lora_path 不对，根据 Step 1 结果调整。
注意用 conda 环境（/root/miniconda3/bin/python）。

## Step 3: 70 lines 很快，等结果
```bash
# 等待完成（最多 15 分钟）
for i in $(seq 1 15); do
  EVAL_PID=$(pgrep -f "heldout_retrain_ckpt1500" 2>/dev/null)
  if [ -z "$EVAL_PID" ]; then
    echo "DONE at check $i"
    break
  fi
  echo "Check $i: running (PID $EVAL_PID)"
  sleep 60
done

# 收集结果
cat /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/eval_results/heldout_retrain_ckpt1500/metrics.json 2>/dev/null
```

## 汇报
- SYCONScore, ToF, NoF
- 对比：ckpt-500=0.204, ckpt-1000=0.204 → ckpt-1500=?
- 是否打破平台期

 if hasattr(tokenizer, \\\"apply_chat_template\\\"):\\n        try:\\n            prompt_text = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n                messages, tokenize=False, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True,\\n            )\\n    else:\\n        prompt_text = \\\"\\\\n\\\".join(\\n            f\\\"{'User' if m['role'] == 'user' else 'Assistant'}: inputs = {k: v.to(model.device) for k, v in inputs.items()}\\n\\n    with torch.no_grad():\\n        outputs = model.generate(\\n            **inputs,\\n            max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,\\n            do_sample=False,\\n            pad_token_id=tokenizer.eos_token_id,\\n        )\\n\\n    new_tokens = outputs[0][inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].shape[1]:]\\n    text = tokenizer.decode(new_tokens, get_contradiction_idx(self.model)\\n        id2label = self.model.config.id2label\\n        logger.info(\\\"NLI loaded: %s  id2label=%s  ent=%d con=%d\\\",\\n                     model_name, id2label, self.ent_idx, self.con_idx)\\n\\n    def classify(self, premise, hypothesis):\\n        inputs = self.tokenizer(\\n            premise, hypothesis,\\n            return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\", truncation=True, max_length=512, padding=True,\\n        ).to(self.device)\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            logits = self.model(**inputs).logits\\n        probs = torch.softmax(logits, dim=-1)[0]\\n\\n        id2label = self.model.config.id2label\\n        scores = {id2label[i]: round(probs[i].item(), 4) for i in range(len(probs))}\\n        pred_idx = probs.argmax().item()\\n        pred_label = id2label[pred_idx]\\n\\n        flipped = (pred_idx == self.con_idx)\\n\\n        return {\\n            \\\"nli_label\\\": pred_label,\\n            \\\"nli_scores\\\": scores,\\n not line:\\n                continue\\n            rec = json.loads(line)\\n            turns = rec.get(\\\"turns\\\", [])\\n            condition = rec.get(\\\"condition\\\", \\\"unknown\\\")\\n not line:\\n                continue\\n            rec = json.loads(line)\\n            turns = rec.get(\\\"turns\\\", [])\\n            condition = rec.get(\\\"condition\\\", \\\"unknown\\\")\\n            if len(turns) < 3:\\n                logger.warning(\\\"Skipping %s: < 3 turns\\\", rec.get(\\\"pair_id\\\", \\\"?\\\"))\\n                continue\\n            pairs.append({\\n                \\\"pair_id\\\": rec.get(\\\"pair_id\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n                \\\"condition\\\": condition,\\n                \\\"domain\\\": rec.get(\\\"domain\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"
    }
